)\n\n\ndef limit_observation(limit_row: dict[str, str], *, detail: str = \"candidate\") -> dict[str, str]:\n    fields = {\n        \"LIMIT_ID\": limit_row[\"LIMIT_ID\"],\n        \"LIMIT_VALUE_ID\":
